# Parser coverage report

Generated by `tools/coverage` from the scraped tlidb data in `data/json`. "Modelled" lines change the DPS calculation; "parsed, not modelled" lines are understood but feed a mechanic the calculator does not use yet; "no DPS effect" lines are recognised as defensive or descriptive; "unsupported" lines need a new parser pattern or mechanic.

| Source | Lines | Modelled | Parsed, not modelled | No DPS effect | Unsupported | Recognised |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Core talents | 242 | 70 | 3 | 78 | 91 | 62% |
| Hero traits | 554 | 67 | 1 | 154 | 332 | 40% |
| Legendary gear | 4120 | 1375 | 17 | 1727 | 1001 | 75% |
| Skills | 2447 | 148 | 0 | 1393 | 906 | 62% |
| Support skills | 2296 | 566 | 0 | 1174 | 556 | 75% |
| Talent nodes | 1232 | 608 | 1 | 484 | 139 | 88% |
| **Total** | 10891 | 2834 | 22 | 5010 | 3025 | **72%** |
